# Component

[java.lang.Object](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/object-0000001054678446) |---ohos.agp.components.Component
    
    public class Component
    extends [Object](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/object-0000001054678446)  
  
---  
Provides basic components for a user interface (UI). 

Each component occupies a rectangular area on the screen and is responsible for drawing and event processing in this area. Component is the base class for controls, which are used to create interactive UI components. Its child class [ComponentContainer](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/componentcontainer-0000001054120043) is the base class for all layouts, which defines layout properties for other components or component groups.

**Since:**

3

#### [h2]SCROLL_NORMAL_STAGE

5

#### [h2]draw

public void draw​([Canvas](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/canvas-0000001054238731) canvas)

7

#### [h2]startDragAndDrop

public final boolean startDragAndDrop​([MimeData](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/mimedata-0000001054678756) data, [Component.DragFeedbackProvider](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component_dragfeedbackprovider-0000001054838668) shadowProvider)

Starts dragging a component. 

There is one way to turn on dragging. [LongClickedListener](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component_longclickedlistener-0000001054119964) need to be set for this component. After [LongClickedListener](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component_longclickedlistener-0000001054119964) listener is invoked before [TouchEvent#PRIMARY_POINT_UP](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/touchevent-0000001054558959#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054558959__PRIMARY_POINT_UP), component is ready for DragAndDrop. startDragAndDrop need to be invoked in [LongClickedListener#onLongClicked](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component_longclickedlistener-0000001054119964#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054119964__onLongClicked-ohos_agp_components_Component-) callback with created [DragFeedbackProvider](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component_dragfeedbackprovider-0000001054838668) and shadowComponent.

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
data  |  Indicates the data carried by the dragged component.  
shadowProvider  |  Indicates the provider of the dragged component's style.  
  
**Returns:**

Returns true if the component is ready for dragging; returns false otherwise.

#### [h2]getComponentParent

public final [ComponentParent](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/componentparent-0000001054558709) getComponentParent​()

Obtains the parent layout of a component. 

Each component must be within a parent layout, or itself must be a root component (or layout).

**Returns:**

Returns the parent layout of the component; returns an empty string if the component itself is a root component (or layout).

#### [h2]addDrawTask

public void addDrawTask​([Component.DrawTask](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component_drawtask-0000001054678675) task)

Adds a drawing task between the foreground and the component content. 

This method invokes [addDrawTask(ohos.agp.components.Component.DrawTask,int)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component-0000001054678683#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678683__addDrawTask-ohos_agp_components_Component_DrawTask-int-), where the value of layer is [DrawTask#BETWEEN_CONTENT_AND_FOREGROUND](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component_drawtask-0000001054678675#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678675__BETWEEN_CONTENT_AND_FOREGROUND).

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
task  |  Indicates the drawing task to add.  
  
**See Also:**

[addDrawTask(DrawTask, int)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component-0000001054678683#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678683__addDrawTask-ohos_agp_components_Component_DrawTask-int-), [invalidate()](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component-0000001054678683#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678683__invalidate--)

#### [h2]addDrawTask

public void addDrawTask​([Component.DrawTask](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component_drawtask-0000001054678675) task, int layer)

Adds a draw task. 

The drawing of each component includes its foreground, content, and background.You can use this method to add a drawing task between the foreground and the content or between the content and the background.

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
task  |  Indicates the drawing task to add.  
layer  |  Indicates the position of the drawing task. This value can only be [DrawTask#BETWEEN_BACKGROUND_AND_CONTENT](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component_drawtask-0000001054678675#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678675__BETWEEN_BACKGROUND_AND_CONTENT) or [DrawTask#BETWEEN_CONTENT_AND_FOREGROUND](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component_drawtask-0000001054678675#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678675__BETWEEN_CONTENT_AND_FOREGROUND).  
  
#### [h2]invalidate

public void invalidate​()

Invalidates a component. 

This method is mainly used to update a component's drawing tasks. Modifying component attributes will automatically trigger a component update.

#### [h2]setContentEnable

public void setContentEnable​(boolean enable)

Sets whether to draw content for a component. 

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
enable  |  Indicates whether to draw the content for a component. true means to draw the component content; false means not to.  
  
#### [h2]getContentEnable

public boolean getContentEnable​()

Obtains whether the content of the component will be drawn. 

**Returns:**

Returns true if the component will be drawn; returns false otherwise. The default value is true.

#### [h2]setFocusedEffect

public void setFocusedEffect​(boolean enabled)

Sets whether this component has a focus effect when it gains focus. 

By default, the focus effect of a component is a focus border. A component may have its own custom focus effect. For example, the custom focus effect of a button can be a focus border with rounded corners. You can also set the background or foreground to achieve the focus effect.

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
enabled  |  Specifies whether to enable the focus effect. The value true (default value) means to enable the focus effect, and false means the opposite.  
  
**Since:**

7

#### [h2]hasFocusedEffect

public boolean hasFocusedEffect​()

Checks whether a focus effect is displayed for this component when it gains focus. 

**Returns:**

Returns true (default value) if a focus effect is displayed; returns false otherwise.

**Since:**

7

#### [h2]createAnimatorProperty

public [AnimatorProperty](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/animatorproperty-0000001054439987) createAnimatorProperty​()

Creates a property animator object associated with a component. 

When you use this method to create an animator object, the current component is automatically set as the target of the animator. The animator only changes the display of the component and the event capturing area. It does not change the layout of the component.

**Returns:**

Returns a new property animator object associated with the current component.

**Since:**

3

#### [h2]setContentPosition

public void setContentPosition​(float x, float y)

Sets positions for drawing and event listening. 

The position of each component relative to the parent layout is indicated using the position of its upper-left corner. This method is used to change the position of a component in the parent layout, as well as the area where the component listens for screen events. This method does not change the layout. It should be used mainly for animation.

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
x  |  Indicates the new X-axis coordinate, in unit of pixels.  
y  |  Indicates the new Y-axis coordinate, in unit of pixels.  
  
#### [h2]setContentPositionX

public void setContentPositionX​(float x)

Sets the x-coordinate for drawing and event listening. 

You can use this method to: 

    * Change the position of a component in the parent layout. Each component takes its upper left corner as its position relative to the parent layout. 
    * Listen for screen events. 

Calling this method does not change the layout. It should be used mainly for animations.

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
x  |  Indicates the x-coordinate to set, in pixels.  
  
**Since:**

4

#### [h2]setContentPositionY

public void setContentPositionY​(float y)

Sets the y-coordinate for drawing and event listening. 

You can use this method to: 

    * Change the position of a component in the parent layout. Each component takes its upper left corner as its position relative to the parent layout. 
    * Listen for screen events. 

Calling this method does not change the layout. It should be used mainly for animations.

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
y  |  Indicates the y-coordinate to set, in pixels.  
  
**Since:**

4

#### [h2]getContentPositionX

public float getContentPositionX​()

Obtains the X-axis coordinate. 

The position of each component relative to the parent layout is indicated using the position of its upper-left corner. This value is not the same as the absolute position of the component's left edge.

**Returns:**

Returns the X-axis coordinate, in unit of pixels.

#### [h2]getContentPositionY

public float getContentPositionY​()

Obtains the Y-axis coordinate. 

The position of each component relative to the parent layout is indicated using the position of its upper-left corner. This value is not the same as the absolute position of the component's top edge.

**Returns:**

Returns the Y-axis coordinate, in unit of pixels.

#### [h2]getContentPosition

public float[] getContentPosition​()

Obtains the positions when drawing. 

The position of each component relative to the parent layout is indicated using the position of its upper-left corner. These values are not the same as the absolute position of the component's upper-left corner.

**Returns:**

Returns the position array, the contents of this array are X-axis and Y-axis coordinate, in unit of pixels.

#### [h2]getLocationOnScreen

public int[] getLocationOnScreen​()

Obtains the absolute position of a component on the screen. 

**Returns:**

Returns an array indicating the absolute position of the component, in unit of pixels.

#### [h2]setWidth

public void setWidth​(int width)

Sets the width of a component. 

This method modifies the width in the LayoutConfig object associated with the component.The actual effect varies depending on the parent layout type.

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
width  |  Indicates the width of the component, in unit of pixels. A value less than 0 means that the component will not be displayed.  
  
#### [h2]setComponentSize

public void setComponentSize​(int width, int height)

Sets the component size. 

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
width  |  Indicates the component width to set, in pixels. If the value is less than 0, the component is not displayed.  
height  |  Indicates the component height, in pixels. If the value is less than 0, the component is not displayed.  
  
**Since:**

4

#### [h2]getComponentSize

public [DimensFloat](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/dimensfloat-0000001060039135) getComponentSize​()

Obtains the component size. 

**Returns:**

Returns a DimensFloat representing the component size, in pixels.

**Since:**

4

#### [h2]getWidth

public int getWidth​()

Obtains the width of a component. 

This method obtains the actual width of the component if it is already bound to a window. Formula: Width = Location of the right edge - Location of the left edge. If the component is not bound to a window, this method returns its width recorded in LayoutConfig.

**Returns:**

Returns the width of the component, in unit of pixels.

#### [h2]setHeight

public void setHeight​(int height)

Sets the height of a component. 

This method modifies the height in the LayoutConfig object associated with the component. The actual effect varies depending on the parent layout type.

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
height  |  Indicates the height of the component, in unit of pixels. A value less than 0 means that the component will not be displayed.  
  
#### [h2]setRotationSensitivity

public void setRotationSensitivity​(float value)

Sets the rotation sensitivity of a component. Each component that supports the rotating crown provides a suitable sensitivity range between ROTATION_SENSITIVITY_LOW and ROTATION_SENSITIVITY_HIGH. The rotation sensitivity of ROTATION_SENSITIVITY_DEFAULT is set by default for each component. You can also adjust the rotation sensitivity appropriately. 

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
value  |  Indicates the rotation sensitivity of the component.  
  
**Since:**

3

#### [h2]getRotationSensitivity

public float getRotationSensitivity​()

Obtains the rotation sensitivity of a component. 

**Returns:**

Returns the rotation sensitivity of the component.

**Since:**

3

#### [h2]getHeight

public int getHeight​()

Obtains the height of a component. 

This method obtains the actual height of the component if it is already bound to a window. Formula: Height = Location of the top edge - Location of the bottom edge. If the component is not bound to a window, this method returns its height recorded in LayoutConfig.

**Returns:**

Returns the height of the component, in unit of pixels.

#### [h2]getLeft

public int getLeft​()

Obtains the left edge position of a component relative to the parent layout. 

This value is the left edge position of the actual layout. It is different from the left edge of the component's displayed area.

**Returns:**

Returns the left edge's relative position, in pixels.

#### [h2]setLeft

public void setLeft​(int left)

Sets the left edge position of a component relative to the parent container. 

This method can be used only by the layout system. We can't use it generally because the property value can be changed at any time by the parent container.

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
left  |  Indicates the left edge's relative position, in pixels.

Parameter Name | Parameter Description

right  |  Indicates the right edge's relative position, in pixels.

Sets the top edge position of a component relative to the parent container.

#### [h2]setComponentPosition

public void setComponentPosition​(int left, int top, int right, int bottom)

Sets the component's position relative to the parent container. 

This method can be used only by the layout system. We can't use it generally because the property values can be changed at any time by the parent container.

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
left  |  Indicates the position of the component's left edge, in pixels.  
top  |  Indicates the position of the component's top edge, in pixels.  
right  |  Indicates the position of the component's right edge, in pixels.  
bottom  |  Indicates the position of the component's bottom edge, in pixels.  
  
**Throws:**

#### [h2]setLayoutConfig

public void setLayoutConfig​([ComponentContainer.LayoutConfig](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/componentcontainer_layoutconfig-0000001054119972) config)

Sets the layout parameters of a component in its parent layout. 

The child class of layout parameters passed in varies depending on the child class of the parent layout. After the component tree that contains the current component is added to a window, the system calculates the position, width, and height of the component in the parent layout based on the ComponentContainer.LayoutConfig parameters and type of the component. The following child classes of layout parameters are supported: 

    * [StackLayout.LayoutConfig](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/stacklayout_layoutconfig-0000001054440007)
    * [DirectionalLayout.LayoutConfig](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/directionallayout_layoutconfig-0000001054678685)
    * [DependentLayout.LayoutConfig](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/dependentlayout_layoutconfig-0000001054558717)
    * [TableLayout.LayoutConfig](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/tablelayout_layoutconfig-0000001054358701)
    * [ComponentContainer.LayoutConfig](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/componentcontainer_layoutconfig-0000001054119972)

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
config  |  Indicates the layout parameters of the component. These parameters cannot be null.  
  
#### [h2]getLayoutConfig

public [ComponentContainer.LayoutConfig](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/componentcontainer_layoutconfig-0000001054119972) getLayoutConfig​()

Obtains the layout parameters associated with a component. 

All components should have layout parameters. They supply parameters to the parent layout of a component specify how the component should be arranged. There are many child classes of [ComponentContainer.LayoutConfig](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/componentcontainer_layoutconfig-0000001054119972), which correspond to different child classes of [ComponentContainer](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/componentcontainer-0000001054120043) that are responsible for arranging their children. If the component is not bound to a parent ComponentContainer or an exception occurs when calling the component is bound to a parent ComponentContainer, this method must not return null. 

**Returns:**

Returns the layout parameters associated with the component; returns null if the component is not bound to a parent ComponentContainer or an exception occurs when calling

#### [h2]setVisibility

public void setVisibility​(int visibility)

Sets the visibility of a component. 

When the parameter value is [VISIBLE](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component-0000001054678683#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678683__VISIBLE), the component occupies space and needs to be rendered. When the parameter value is [INVISIBLE](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component-0000001054678683#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678683__INVISIBLE), the component occupies space but does not need to be rendered. When the parameter value is [HIDE](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component-0000001054678683#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678683__HIDE), the component occupies no space and does not need to be rendered. 

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
visibility  |  The value is one of the following: [VISIBLE](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component-0000001054678683#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678683__VISIBLE), [INVISIBLE](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component-0000001054678683#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678683__INVISIBLE), or [HIDE](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component-0000001054678683#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678683__HIDE).  
  
#### [h2]getVisibility

public int getVisibility​()

Obtains the visibility of a component. 

A component can have three visibility states: 

    * [VISIBLE](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component-0000001054678683#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678683__VISIBLE): The component occupies space and needs to be rendered.
    * [INVISIBLE](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component-0000001054678683#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678683__INVISIBLE): The component occupies space but does not need to be rendered.
    * [HIDE](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component-0000001054678683#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678683__HIDE): The component occupies no space and does not need to be rendered.

**Returns:**

Returns the visibility state of the component. The value can be one of the following: [VISIBLE](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component-0000001054678683#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678683__VISIBLE), [INVISIBLE](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component-0000001054678683#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678683__INVISIBLE), or [HIDE](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component-0000001054678683#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678683__HIDE).

**See Also:**

[setVisibility(int visibility)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component-0000001054678683#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678683__setVisibility-int-)

#### [h2]notifyAccessibility

public void notifyAccessibility​(int eventType)

Triggers an accessibility event specified by this component. 

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
eventType  |  Indicates the accessibility event to trigger. The value can be any of the following: 
    * 0x00000001: A component such as a button is clicked.
    * 0x00000002: A component is long pressed.
    * 0x00000004: A component is selected.
    * 0x00000008: A component is in focus.
    * 0x00000010: A text is changed.
    * 0x00000020: The content of a window is changed.
    * 0x00001000: A component stops scrolling.
    * 0x00004000: The content of a component is read. 
    * 0x00008000: The accessibility focus is obtained.
    * 0x00010000: The accessibility focus is cleared.
    * 0x01000000: A component starts scrolling.  
  
**Since:**

4

#### [h2]isBoundToWindow

public boolean isBoundToWindow​()

Checks whether the component has been bound to a window. 

Each window creates a component tree. Each component that needs to be displayed in the window must be bound to this tree.Adding a component to the component tree is equivalent to binding a component to a window.

**Returns:**

Returns true if the component has been bound to a window; returns false otherwise.

**Since:**

4

#### [h2]isComponentDisplayed

public boolean isComponentDisplayed​()

Checks whether the component can be displayed. 

This is different from the visibility of the component. For example, for a component whose visibility state is [VISIBLE](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component-0000001054678683#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678683__VISIBLE), if its size is 0 or if its parent layout cannot be displayed, the component still cannot be displayed on the screen. On the contrary, even for a component that is completely transparent, this method returns true if the component can be displayed on the screen simply by changing its transparency color. 

**Returns:**

Returns true if the component can be displayed; returns false otherwise.

**Since:**

4

public void setDraggedListener​(int dragMode, [Component.DraggedListener](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component_draggedlistener-0000001054238705) listener)

Registers a listener for drag events of a component. 

All registered observers will be notified of drag events dispatched to this component. If listener is null, drag events will be canceled. One component can own several identical drag recognizers, and the user-defined drag recognizer takes precedence over the internal drag recognizer.

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
dragMode  |  Indicates the current drag mode. The value can be one of the following: [DRAG_HORIZONTAL_VERTICAL](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component-0000001054678683#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678683__DRAG_HORIZONTAL_VERTICAL), [DRAG_HORIZONTAL](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component-0000001054678683#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678683__DRAG_HORIZONTAL), [DRAG_VERTICAL](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component-0000001054678683#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678683__DRAG_VERTICAL)  
listener  |  Indicates the listener for drag events.  
  
**Since:**

3

#### [h2]setDragAcceptAngle

public void setDragAcceptAngle​(float degree)

Sets the deflection angle for a horizontal or vertical drag recognizer. 

The deflection angle refers to the included angle between the dragging direction and the principal direction, which is horizontal regarding a horizontal drag recognizer or vertical regarding a vertical drag recognizer. If the dragging displacement on the touchscreen projected onto the principal direction exceeds the preset threshold (8 vp) but is less than twice the threshold, the drag recognizer calculates the deflection angle and assumes that dragging starts only when the calculated angle is less than the one you set via this method. If the dragging displacement exceeds twice the threshold, the drag recognizer assumes that dragging starts, regardless of the deflection angle you set. This method takes effect only on a drag recognizer whose dragMode is set to [DRAG_HORIZONTAL](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component-0000001054678683#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678683__DRAG_HORIZONTAL) or [DRAG_VERTICAL](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component-0000001054678683#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678683__DRAG_VERTICAL) via [setDraggedListener(int, ohos.agp.components.Component.DraggedListener)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component-0000001054678683#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678683__setDraggedListener-int-ohos_agp_components_Component_DraggedListener-). The default deflection angle is 26.57 degrees.

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
degree  |  Indicates the deflection angle to set. The value ranges from 15.0 to 75.0 degrees.  
  
**Since:**

5

**See Also:**

[getDragAcceptAngle()](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component-0000001054678683#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678683__getDragAcceptAngle--), [setDraggedListener(int dragMode, DraggedListener listener)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component-0000001054678683#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678683__setDraggedListener-int-ohos_agp_components_Component_DraggedListener-)

#### [h2]setClipToContourEnabled

public void setClipToContourEnabled​(boolean enable)

Sets whether the [Contour](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/contour-0000001078032066) object of a component can be used for content clipping. Clipping is supported only when the [Contour](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/contour-0000001078032066) object takes the form of a right-angled rectangle, a rounded rectangle, or a circle. The part beyond the [Contour](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/contour-0000001078032066) object will be cropped. 

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
enable  |  Indicates whether clipping is supported. Value true indicates that clipping is supported; value false indicates the opposite.  
  
**Since:**

7

#### [h2]simulateClick

public boolean simulateClick​()

Performs a click event in a component. 

This method calls the OnClickListener created for the component. The observer registered by the [setClickedListener(ohos.agp.components.Component.ClickedListener)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component-0000001054678683#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678683__setClickedListener-ohos_agp_components_Component_ClickedListener-) method will be notified. After the callback is executed, the system passes a lifting event to the click event listener.

**Returns:**

Returns true if the callback function is executed successfully.

**Since:**

4

#### [h2]performScale

public boolean performScale​()

Performs a scale event in a component. 

This method invokes the callback for scale events of the control. Observers registered by the [setScaledListener(ohos.agp.components.Component.ScaledListener)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component-0000001054678683#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678683__setScaledListener-ohos_agp_components_Component_ScaledListener-) method will be notified. When the callback is executed, the system will transfer the scale event to the scale listener.

**Returns:**

Returns true if the callback function is executed successfully.

#### [h2]simulateDrag

public boolean simulateDrag​()

Performs a drag event of a component. 

This method invokes the callback for drag events of the control. Observers registered by the [setDraggedListener(int,ohos.agp.components.Component.DraggedListener)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component-0000001054678683#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678683__setDraggedListener-int-ohos_agp_components_Component_DraggedListener-) method will be notified. When the callback is executed, the system will transfer the drag event to the drag listener.

**Returns:**

Returns true if the callback function is executed successfully.

**Since:**

4

#### [h2]callOnClick

public boolean callOnClick​()

Calls the assigned OnClickListener. 

Unlike [simulateClick()](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component-0000001054678683#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678683__simulateClick--), this method only notifies observers registered using the [setClickedListener(ohos.agp.components.Component.ClickedListener)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component-0000001054678683#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678683__setClickedListener-ohos_agp_components_Component_ClickedListener-) method, but does not perform any actual operation on the component itself.

**Returns:**

Returns true if the assigned OnClickListener is called successfully.

#### [h2]executeLongClick

public boolean executeLongClick​()

Performs a long-click event in a component. 

This method calls the OnLongClickListener created for the component. Observers registered by the [setLongClickedListener(ohos.agp.components.Component.LongClickedListener)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component-0000001054678683#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678683__setLongClickedListener-ohos_agp_components_Component_LongClickedListener-) method will be notified. After the callback is executed, the system passes a lifting event to the long-click event listener.

**Returns:**

Returns true if the callback function is executed successfully.

**Since:**

4

#### [h2]executeDoubleClick

public boolean executeDoubleClick​()

Executes the double-click event occurring in this component. 

This method calls the OnDoubleClickListener created for this component. Observers registered by the [setDoubleClickedListener(ohos.agp.components.Component.DoubleClickedListener)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component-0000001054678683#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678683__setDoubleClickedListener-ohos_agp_components_Component_DoubleClickedListener-) method will be notified. After the callback is invoked, the system passes an event indicating the end of a double-click event to the listener set by [setDoubleClickedListener(ohos.agp.components.Component.DoubleClickedListener)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component-0000001054678683#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678683__setDoubleClickedListener-ohos_agp_components_Component_DoubleClickedListener-).

**Returns:**

Returns true if the operation is successful; returns false otherwise.

**Since:**

5

#### [h2]setClickable

public void setClickable​(boolean clickable)

Enables or disables click events for the component. 

If a component is clickable, its state will change to pressed upon every click. Child classes should set the component to clickable to visually react to user clicks. If a component is not clickable, it should not be able to receive focus. If it has already received focus, the focus should be cleared immediately.

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
clickable  |  true means to make the component clickable, and false means the opposite.  
  
**See Also:**

[isClickable()](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component-0000001054678683#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678683__isClickable--)

#### [h2]isClickable

public boolean isClickable​()

Returns whether the component responds to click events or not. 

If a component is not clickable, it cannot receive focus.

**Returns:**

Returns true if the component is clickable; returns false if it is not.

**See Also:**

[setClickable(boolean)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component-0000001054678683#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678683__setClickable-boolean-)

Sets the enabled state of the component. 

The interpretation of the enabled state varies depending on the child class. If a component is not enabled, it should not acquire focus.

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
enabled  |  true means the component is enabled, and false means the opposite.

Obtains the enabled state of the component. The default state is enabled.

#### [h2]setFocusable

public void setFocusable​(int focusable)

Sets whether the component can receive focus. 

The value [FOCUS_ENABLE](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component-0000001054678683#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678683__FOCUS_ENABLE) means the component can receive focus. The value [FOCUS_ADAPTABLE](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component-0000001054678683#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678683__FOCUS_ADAPTABLE) means to let the framework to determine whether the component can receive focus based on the component's interactivity. This is the default value. The value [FOCUS_DISABLE](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component-0000001054678683#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678683__FOCUS_DISABLE) ensures that the component is also not focusable in touch mode. Each component uses a state bit to indicate whether it is focusable. If a component is not clickable, this state bit cannot be changed to indicate focusable. 

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
focusable  |  Indicates whether the component can gain focus. The value can be [FOCUS_DISABLE](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component-0000001054678683#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678683__FOCUS_DISABLE), [FOCUS_ENABLE](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component-0000001054678683#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678683__FOCUS_ENABLE), or [FOCUS_ADAPTABLE](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component-0000001054678683#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678683__FOCUS_ADAPTABLE).

**Returns:**

Returns the focusable setting of the component. The value can be any of the following: [FOCUS_DISABLE](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component-0000001054678683#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678683__FOCUS_DISABLE), [FOCUS_ENABLE](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component-0000001054678683#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678683__FOCUS_ENABLE), or [FOCUS_ADAPTABLE](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component-0000001054678683#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678683__FOCUS_ADAPTABLE).

#### [h2]setLongClickable

public void setLongClickable​(boolean longClickable)

Sets whether the component is long clickable. 

A long-clickable component reacts to long click events, that is, the user holding down a button for a longer duration than a tap.

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
longClickable  |  Indicates whether the component is long clickable.  
  
**See Also:**

4

[setLongClickable(boolean)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component-0000001054678683#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678683__setLongClickable-boolean-)

Calling this method may change the appearance of the component, if a different appearance has been set for such state.

---|---

#### [h2]isPressed

public boolean isPressed​()

Returns whether the component is currently in pressed state. 

If the [setPressState(boolean)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component-0000001054678683#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678683__setPressState-boolean-) function has not been invoked, only a clickable component can enter the pressed state.

**Returns:**

Returns true if the component is in pressed state; returns false otherwise.

#### [h2]setSelected

public void setSelected​(boolean selected)

Sets the selection state of the component. 

A component can be selected or deselected. Note that being selected is not the same as in focus. components are typically selected in the context of an AdapterComponent like [ListContainer](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/listcontainer-0000001054678718); a selected component is highlighted. 

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
selected  |  Indicates the selected state of the component. true indicates the component is selected.  
  
#### [h2]isSelected

public boolean isSelected​()

Obtains the selection state of the component. 

**Returns:**

Returns true if the component is selected; returns false otherwise.

4

#### [h2]requestFocus

public boolean requestFocus​()

Requests focus for the current component or one of its child components. 

A component will not be able to actually take focus in any of the following situations: the component is not focusable ([isFocusable()](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component-0000001054678683#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678683__isFocusable--) returns false); or the component is not focusable in touch mode ([isTouchFocusable()](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component-0000001054678683#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678683__isTouchFocusable--) returns false); or the component is invisible, disabled, or has not acquired space for drawing. 

**Returns:**

Returns true if the component or one of its child components has taken focus; returns false otherwise.

Requests giving up focus.

#### [h2]setBindStateChangedListener

public void setBindStateChangedListener​([Component.BindStateChangedListener](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component_bindstatechangedlistener-0000001054600115) listener)

Adds a listener for bind state changes. 

To display a component on the screen, you must add it to the component tree of a window. Each component has a listener list that has implemented the [BindStateChangedListener](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component_bindstatechangedlistener-0000001054600115) interface. When a component is bound to or unbound from a window, the [BindStateChangedListener#onComponentBoundToWindow(Component)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component_bindstatechangedlistener-0000001054600115#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054600115__onComponentBoundToWindow-ohos_agp_components_Component-) and [BindStateChangedListener#onComponentUnboundFromWindow(Component)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component_bindstatechangedlistener-0000001054600115#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054600115__onComponentUnboundFromWindow-ohos_agp_components_Component-) methods of this interface are called back, respectively. This method adds listeners specified by the input parameter to the listener list. 

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
listener  |  Indicates the listener to add.  
  
**Since:**

4

**See Also:**

[removeBindStateChangedListener(BindStateChangedListener)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component-0000001054678683#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678683__removeBindStateChangedListener-ohos_agp_components_Component_BindStateChangedListener-)

#### [h2]removeBindStateChangedListener

public void removeBindStateChangedListener​([Component.BindStateChangedListener](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component_bindstatechangedlistener-0000001054600115) listener)

Removes a listener for bind state changes. 

To display a component on the screen, you must add it to the component tree of a window. Each component has a listener list that has implemented the [BindStateChangedListener](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component_bindstatechangedlistener-0000001054600115) interface. When a component is bound to or unbound from a window, the [BindStateChangedListener#onComponentBoundToWindow(Component)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component_bindstatechangedlistener-0000001054600115#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054600115__onComponentBoundToWindow-ohos_agp_components_Component-) (Component)} and [BindStateChangedListener#onComponentUnboundFromWindow(Component)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component_bindstatechangedlistener-0000001054600115#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054600115__onComponentUnboundFromWindow-ohos_agp_components_Component-) methods of this interface are called back, respectively. This method removes listeners specified by the input parameter from the listener list. 

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
listener  |  Indicates the listener to remove.  
  
**Since:**

4

**See Also:**

[setBindStateChangedListener(BindStateChangedListener)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component-0000001054678683#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678683__setBindStateChangedListener-ohos_agp_components_Component_BindStateChangedListener-)

The focus border is displayed only when the component is in focus and the focus border is enabled.

4

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
padding  |  Indicates the padding to set, in pixels. Any value smaller than 0 is invalid.  
  
**Since:**

4

The focus border is displayed only when the component is in focus and the focus border is enabled.

---|---  
radius  |  Indicates the radius to set. This value must be greater than 0, and in this case the focus border is a rounded rectangle or a circle.

4

---|---

**Since:**

4

[setScaleX(float)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component-0000001054678683#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678683__setScaleX-float-), [getScaleY()](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component-0000001054678683#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678683__getScaleY--), [getScaleX()](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component-0000001054678683#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678683__getScaleX--)

#### [h2]setScale

public void setScale​(float x, float y)

Sets the component's scale factor around the pivot. 

The scale factor is used to control the scale effect of the component based on its original size.

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
x  |  Indicates the scale factor in the x-axis direction.  
y  |  Indicates the scale factor in the y-axis direction.  
  
**Since:**

4

#### [h2]setTranslationX

public void setTranslationX​(float translationX)

Sets the horizontal position of the component relative to its left position. 

This value is the distance at which the component has been moved horizontally relative to the [getLeft()](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component-0000001054678683#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678683__getLeft--) position. The rendering position will need to be adjusted accordingly. 

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
translationX  |  Indicates the horizontal shift of the component relative to its left position, in pixels.  
  
**See Also:**

[setTranslationY(float)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component-0000001054678683#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678683__setTranslationY-float-), [getTranslationX()](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component-0000001054678683#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678683__getTranslationX--), [getTranslationY()](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component-0000001054678683#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678683__getTranslationY--)

#### [h2]setTranslationY

public void setTranslationY​(float translationY)

Sets the vertical position of the component relative to its top position. 

This value is the distance at which the component has been moved vertically relative to the [getTop()](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component-0000001054678683#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678683__getTop--) position. The rendering position will need to be adjusted accordingly. 

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
translationY  |  Indicates the vertical shift of the component relative to its top position, in pixels.  
  
**See Also:**

[setTranslationX(float)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component-0000001054678683#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678683__setTranslationX-float-), [getTranslationX()](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component-0000001054678683#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678683__getTranslationX--), [getTranslationY()](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component-0000001054678683#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678683__getTranslationY--)

Allows to get local visible Rect (related to component's parent) 

**Parameters:**

---|---

**Since:**

4

#### [h2]getVisibleRectOnScreen

public boolean getVisibleRectOnScreen​([Rect](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/rect-0000001054558757) rect, int[] position)

Obtains the visible rectangle area of a component in relative to the upper left corner of the screen. The visible area is an area of the component that has not been cropped by its parent component. 

Note that the visible area obtained by using this method is the visible area of the current frame. If you want to obtain the visible area of the next frame, you need to call this method in [LayoutRefreshedListener#onRefreshed(Component)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component_layoutrefreshedlistener-0000001054120035#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054120035__onRefreshed-ohos_agp_components_Component-).

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
rect  |  Indicates the visible rectangle area. When this method returns true, this parameter saves the visible rectangle area of the component in relative to the upper left corner of the screen. When this method returns false, the top, bottom, left, and right coordinates of the visible rectangle are all 0.  
position  |  Indicates the position. When this method returns true, this parameter saves the coordinates of the component in relative to the upper left corner of the screen. When this method returns false, the coordinates saved in this parameter are all 0.  
  
**Returns:**

Returns true if the current component has a visible rectangle area on its parent component; returns false otherwise.

**Since:**

7

public [Matrix](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/matrix-0000001054518768) getMatrix​()

**Returns:**

Returns the conversion matrix of the current component.

7

#### [h2]isIdentityMatrix

public boolean isIdentityMatrix​()

Checks whether the conversion matrix of the current component is an identity matrix. You can use this method to check whether the component has been translated, rotated, or scaled. 

**Returns:**

Returns true if the current component is an identity matrix; returns false otherwise.

**Since:**

7

#### [h2]getWindowVisibleRect

public boolean getWindowVisibleRect​([Rect](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/rect-0000001054558757) visibleRect)

Obtain the visible rect of the window where the component is located. The visible rect is an area in which a window can display content to user, and it can be changed by status bar, navigation bar and input method. 

The visible rect of the window can only be obtained by root Component in the slice.

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
visibleRect  |  Rect container to save visible rect values of window  
  
**Returns:**

true if the component contains visible Rect, false otherwise

**Since:**

5

#### [h2]findComponentById

public <T extends [Component](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component-0000001054678683)> T findComponentById​(int id)

Finds the component with the given ID. 

If the ID of the current component itself matches the given ID, the current component is returned. Otherwise, null is returned.

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
id  |  Indicates a unique identifier of a component.  
  
**Returns:**

Returns the component whose ID matches the given ID; returns null if no component is found.

#### [h2]findFocus

public [Component](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component-0000001054678683) findFocus​()

Obtains the component that currently has focus among its descendant components and itself. 

If the component is a simple component and currently has focus, the component is returned. Otherwise, null is returned. If the component has descendants and one of the descendants has focus, the focused descendant is returned. If no descendant has focus, null is returned. 

**Returns:**

Returns the focused component; or null if no focused component can be found.

#### [h2]findNextFocusableComponent

public [Component](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component-0000001054678683) findNextFocusableComponent​(int direction)

Finds the next component to take focus. 

The method returns the next focusable component in a given direction from the component that currently has focus. If the next focusable component is not specified, the closest sibling component in the given direction is returned. 

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
direction  |  Indicates the focus moving direction. The value can be one of the following: [FOCUS_SIDE_LEFT](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component-0000001054678683#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678683__FOCUS_SIDE_LEFT), [FOCUS_SIDE_TOP](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component-0000001054678683#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678683__FOCUS_SIDE_TOP), [FOCUS_SIDE_RIGHT](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component-0000001054678683#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678683__FOCUS_SIDE_RIGHT), [FOCUS_SIDE_BOTTOM](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component-0000001054678683#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678683__FOCUS_SIDE_BOTTOM),[FOCUS_NEXT](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component-0000001054678683#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678683__FOCUS_NEXT), or [FOCUS_PREVIOUS](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component-0000001054678683#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678683__FOCUS_PREVIOUS).  
  
**Returns:**

Returns the next component to receive focus, or null if no such component can be found.

**Since:**

4

#### [h2]findRequestNextFocus

public boolean findRequestNextFocus​(int side)

Finds the next component to gain focus and requests focus for it. 

This method finds the next focusable component in a given direction from the currently focused component. If the next focusable component is not found, the closest sibling component in the given direction will be returned.

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
side  |  Indicates the focus moving direction. The value can be: [FOCUS_SIDE_LEFT](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component-0000001054678683#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678683__FOCUS_SIDE_LEFT), [FOCUS_SIDE_TOP](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component-0000001054678683#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678683__FOCUS_SIDE_TOP), [FOCUS_SIDE_RIGHT](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component-0000001054678683#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678683__FOCUS_SIDE_RIGHT), [FOCUS_SIDE_BOTTOM](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component-0000001054678683#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678683__FOCUS_SIDE_BOTTOM), [FOCUS_NEXT](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component-0000001054678683#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678683__FOCUS_NEXT), or [FOCUS_PREVIOUS](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component-0000001054678683#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678683__FOCUS_PREVIOUS).  
  
**Returns:**

Returns true if the component or one of its child components has gained focus; returns false otherwise.

**Since:**

3

#### [h2]setUserNextFocus

public void setUserNextFocus​(int side, int id)

Sets the next component to give focus to. 

The next component to give focus to must be another child of the same parent. Otherwise, a null pointer will be obtained when [findNextFocusableComponent(int)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component-0000001054678683#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678683__findNextFocusableComponent-int-) is called.

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
side  |  Indicates the focus moving direction. The value can be one of the following: [FOCUS_SIDE_LEFT](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component-0000001054678683#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678683__FOCUS_SIDE_LEFT), [FOCUS_SIDE_TOP](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component-0000001054678683#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678683__FOCUS_SIDE_TOP), [FOCUS_SIDE_RIGHT](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component-0000001054678683#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678683__FOCUS_SIDE_RIGHT), [FOCUS_SIDE_BOTTOM](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component-0000001054678683#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678683__FOCUS_SIDE_BOTTOM),[FOCUS_NEXT](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component-0000001054678683#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678683__FOCUS_NEXT), or [FOCUS_PREVIOUS](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component-0000001054678683#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678683__FOCUS_PREVIOUS).  
id  |  Indicates the ID of the next component to give focus to.  
  
#### [h2]getUserNextFocus

public int getUserNextFocus​(int side)

Obtains the ID of the next component to give focus to. 

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
side  |  Indicates the focus moving direction. The value can be one of the following: [FOCUS_SIDE_LEFT](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component-0000001054678683#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678683__FOCUS_SIDE_LEFT), [FOCUS_SIDE_TOP](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component-0000001054678683#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678683__FOCUS_SIDE_TOP), [FOCUS_SIDE_RIGHT](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component-0000001054678683#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678683__FOCUS_SIDE_RIGHT), [FOCUS_SIDE_BOTTOM](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component-0000001054678683#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678683__FOCUS_SIDE_BOTTOM),[FOCUS_NEXT](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component-0000001054678683#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678683__FOCUS_NEXT), or [FOCUS_PREVIOUS](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component-0000001054678683#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678683__FOCUS_PREVIOUS).  
  
**Returns:**

Returns the ID of the next component to give focus to; returns -1 if no such component is set.

3# Component

[java.lang.Object](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/object-0000001054678446) |---ohos.agp.components.Component
    
    public class Component
    extends [Object](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/object-0000001054678446)  
  
---  
Provides basic components for a user interface (UI). 

Each component occupies a rectangular area on the screen and is responsible for drawing and event processing in this area. Component is the base class for controls, which are used to create interactive UI components. Its child class [ComponentContainer](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/componentcontainer-0000001054120043) is the base class for all layouts, which defines layout properties for other components or component groups.

**Since:**

3

#### [h2]setMode

public void setMode​(int mode) Sets the showing mode of this component. When the parameter value is [INHERITED_MODE](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component-0000001054678683#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678683__INHERITED_MODE) (default value), the parent component's showing mode is used. When the parameter value is [RECT_MODE](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component-0000001054678683#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678683__RECT_MODE), the content of this component is displayed in a rectangle. When the parameter value is [OVAL_MODE](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component-0000001054678683#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678683__OVAL_MODE), the content of this component is displayed in an ellipse. **Parameters:** | Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
mode  |  Indicates the showing mode of this component.  
  
**Since:**

3

**See Also:**

[getMode()](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component-0000001054678683#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678683__getMode--), [getModeResolved()](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component-0000001054678683#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678683__getModeResolved--)

#### [h2]getMode

public int getMode​()

Obtains the showing mode of this component. 

**Returns:**

the showing mode. One of the values: [INHERITED_MODE](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component-0000001054678683#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678683__INHERITED_MODE), [RECT_MODE](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component-0000001054678683#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678683__RECT_MODE), [OVAL_MODE](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component-0000001054678683#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678683__OVAL_MODE)

**Since:**

3

**See Also:**

[setMode(int)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component-0000001054678683#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678683__setMode-int-), [getModeResolved()](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component-0000001054678683#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678683__getModeResolved--)

#### [h2]setScrollBarMode

public void setScrollBarMode​(int scrollBarMode)

Sets the mode of the scrollbar that is being displayed. If no mode is set for the scrollbar, the showing component's mode is used. 

When scrollBarMode is set to [UNSPECIFIED_SCROLL_BAR_MODE](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component-0000001054678683#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678683__UNSPECIFIED_SCROLL_BAR_MODE), the scrollbar uses the same mode as the showing component. When this parameter is set to [RECT_SCROLL_BAR_MODE](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component-0000001054678683#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678683__RECT_SCROLL_BAR_MODE), the scrollbar is displayed as a rectangle. When this parameter is [OVAL_SCROLL_BAR_MODE](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component-0000001054678683#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678683__OVAL_SCROLL_BAR_MODE), the scrollbar is displayed as an ellipse.

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
scrollBarMode  |  Indicates the mode of the scrollbar to set.  
  
**Since:**

7

**See Also:**

[getScrollBarMode()](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component-0000001054678683#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678683__getScrollBarMode--)

#### [h2]getScrollBarMode

public int getScrollBarMode​()

Obtains the mode of the scrollbar that is being displayed. 

**Returns:**

Returns the scrollbar mode, which can be [UNSPECIFIED_SCROLL_BAR_MODE](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component-0000001054678683#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678683__UNSPECIFIED_SCROLL_BAR_MODE), [RECT_SCROLL_BAR_MODE](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component-0000001054678683#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678683__RECT_SCROLL_BAR_MODE), or [OVAL_SCROLL_BAR_MODE](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component-0000001054678683#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678683__OVAL_SCROLL_BAR_MODE).

**Since:**

7

**See Also:**

[setScrollBarMode(int)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component-0000001054678683#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678683__setScrollBarMode-int-)

#### [h2]getModeResolved

public int getModeResolved​()

Obtains the display mode of the device holding this component. 

**Returns:**

Returns the display mode, either [RECT_MODE](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component-0000001054678683#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678683__RECT_MODE) or [OVAL_MODE](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component-0000001054678683#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678683__OVAL_MODE).

**Since:**

4

**See Also:**

[setMode(int)](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component-0000001054678683#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678683__setMode-int-), [getMode()](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component-0000001054678683#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678683__getMode--)

#### [h2]setCenterZoomFactor

public void setCenterZoomFactor​(float scaleX, float scaleY)

Sets the zoom factor for scaling the center component item. 

The value can be [DEFAULT_SCALE](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/component-0000001054678683#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001054678683__DEFAULT_SCALE), greater than 1, less than 1, representing the default zoom factor, zoom-out factor, and zoom-in factor respectively.

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
scaleX  |  Indicates the zoom factor for scaling the center component item on X-axis.  
scaleY  |  Indicates the zoom factor for scaling the center component item on Y-axis.  
  
**Since:**

3

#### [h2]getCenterZoomFactor

public float[] getCenterZoomFactor​()

Obtains the zoom factor for scaling the center component item. 

**Returns:**

Returns the array of the X-axis and Y-axis coordinates of the zoom factor.

**Since:**

3

#### [h2]setBoundaryFadeEffectEnable

[@Deprecated](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/deprecated-0000001054838412) public void setBoundaryFadeEffectEnable​(boolean enable)

Deprecated. This change is effective since API version 6. Replaced by enableFadeEffect(FadeEffectEnum) or disableFadeEffect(FadeEffectEnum)

Sets whether to display the boundary of the component's fade-effect area. 

**Parameters:**

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
enable  |  Specifies whether to display the boundary of the component's fade-effect area. The value true indicates that the boundary is displayed, and false indicates the opposite case.  
  
**Since:**

5

#### [h2]isBoundaryFadeEffectEnable

#### [h2]getBoundaryFadeEffectLeftRate

public float getBoundaryFadeEffectLeftRate​()

4

#### [h2]enableSaveState

public void enableSaveState​(boolean isEnabled)

Sets whether this component and its child components can save their states.

Parameter Name | Parameter Description  
---|---  
isEnabled  |  Specifies whether this component and its child components can save their states. The value true means that they can save the states, and false means the opposite.

7

#### [h2]isSaveStateEnabled

public boolean isSaveStateEnabled​()

Checks whether this component and its child components can save their states.

Returns true if this component and its child components can save their states; returns false otherwise.

7

#### [h2]onSaveState

protected [Sequenceable](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/sequenceable-0000001054239001) onSaveState​()

Called when the states of this component and its child components are to be saved. 

If a child class wants to save a specific state, you can override this method.

**Returns:**

Returns a [Sequenceable](https://developer.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/documentation/doc-references/sequenceable-0000001054239001) object that holds the state information.

**Since:**

7
